# HTKPA — RE cycle log (append-only)

**Purpose:** tiny, durable handoff after **every** reverse-engineering work cycle. This is **not** a replacement for [the current full status](../../docs/NAM_CLO_HTKPA_RESEARCH_STATUS_2026-10-13.md), stage code/evidence or occasional DOC-SYNC. Read the **last entry first** on resumption; preserve earlier entries unless a correction is explicitly noted.

**Original target:** Windows `5868USB.dll`, SHA-256 `fa8b3613a75de69deb49a9e72dce453312e4814a5c99bb6ea2e8a9e80debd5fa`. Source-first static RE. **No native DLL/RAM or end-to-end NAM→CLO parity** is implied by individual unit-test passes.

## Minimal per-cycle contract

After **each** research cycle, including one with a negative result, append **one entry (5–9 compact lines)** to this file **in the same checkpoint commit where feasible**. Keep new research time for disassembly, not prose. Record:

1. **When / stage / result:** date, cycle label and whether the hypothesis succeeded, failed or remains open.
2. **Proof:** exact DLL address/range; what instructions establish; distinguish **SOURCE**, **DERIVED**, **SYNTHETIC**, **NATIVE** (only if genuinely measured).
3. **Verification:** runnable repo-relative script/test, measured outcome or precise failure and its environment.
4. **Unresolved / blockers:** do not hide assumptions or interpret synthetic tests as vendor parity.
5. **Next:** one exact code range and question; relevant SHA/commit/file paths when available.

Avoid re-reporting old stages, lengthy tutorials, version-per-paragraph files, repeated full source listings and new production code. If an earlier conclusion is overturned, append an explicit **CORRECTION** and point to the affected entry rather than silently rewriting history. During the next deliberate DOC-SYNC, integrate durable facts into the long status and keep this log as the chronological trail.

## 2026-10-13 — Stage23S · FIR FFT multiply branch and unresolved representation

This is *not* generally the same mathematical operation. DC/Nyquist use separate real products `0x18000C577–0x18000C590`.

## 2026-10-13 — Stage23Z · POW exponent correction in FIR window

- **SOURCE-CORRECTED / NATIVE OFFLINE RECHECK:** Original SHA-pinned DLL; **24 new exact PE instruction pins** plus verified import thunks. Generator `0x180014810` invokes `0x18017E152` at `0x18001496E` and `0x1800149C4`: that thunk resolves via IAT `0x18018BA08` to **`pow`**, *not* `sin` (which imports at `0x18018BA18`). `XMM1` is exponent from generator context `+0x20`, populated from absolute `xmm9` under `0x18000B8AF/0x18000B8C1`. `0x180008FD0` supplies Bessel-window base.
- **CORRECTION to Stage23I/Y:** Stage23I modeled `sin(gamma) * window`, omitting **`pow(window, gamma)`**, with source-derived `gamma=0.15823713794954866`, Bessel parameter `beta=125`. No native-target fitting performed. The old candidate reproduces published original SHA256 `ff4b5875…`; corrected independent source model SHA256 `de6c94e0…` (still **not** native SHA `649adeb7…`).
- **NUMERIC (private native 1417-tap pre-FFT):** max error dropped `1.6025453961367097e-06 → 9.282550449007586e-10` (**1726×**); full-4096 RMS `2.8553058553229254e-07 → 2.1566018388829852e-10` (**1324×**). ## 2026-10-13 — Stage27 output router RE: stride one selects memcpy; next capture prepared

- **SOURCE:** Original SHA-pinned `5868USB.dll` re-disassembled at `0x18000C6FC–0x18000C838`. `C6FC` loads `state+0x54` into ECX; `C6FF` loads signed `state+0x2c` into R10. When ECX>0, `C707–C754` calculates shifts and copies contiguous doubles with memcpy. Otherwise `C759` selects a stride branch: if R10>1, `C767–C822` copies individual qwords separated by `R10*8` bytes while updating phase `state+0x90`; if R10<=1, `C824–C838` copies a contiguous double region with memcpy. The `state+0x2c` field is therefore **source-proven output sample stride**, not a fractional interpolator's period.
- **NATIVE link:** Stage26 observed eight `state+0x2c=1` and `state+0x54=0`, as well as four hits at `C838`: this is the exact direct-copy branch. The previously reported eight-sample output snippets are copies of the already computed FIR buffer, not evidence for a downstream 24-tap fractional interpolator.
- **CAPTURE PREP (not executed):** `tools/frida_examples/htkpa_stage27_output_routing.json` hooks branch decision (`C6FC`) to read stride, shift, skip and phase; required source `f64_slice` before memcpy (`C838`); optional probes at strided inner copy `C80D` and shifted copy `C745`. The first 16 source doubles are bounded, retaining IEEE bits. No output after volatile memcpy register call is inferred. Existing WSL wrapper accepts `FRIDA_CONFIG=htkpa_stage27_output_routing.json`.
- **NEXT:** one Windows run to verify stride/phase and source snippets. Separately locate the actual fractional interpolation call (if present in this pipeline) by source call-graph tracing; avoid assuming output router implements it. No complete 44.1→48 kHz parity claimed.

## 2026-10-14 — Stage40 USER WSL double-confirmed output BUT ORIGINAL COMPARISON INVALID (fixed)

- **USER EVIDENCE:** First Stage40 with full private `stage23w_native(1).zip` and second Stage40 with compact `stage40_native_fir_fixtures.zip` produced exactly the same filter hashes/results for both 1417- and 1543-tap configurations. r8brain pinned `cb2abb9977efe2471979b380ed95daa56ab4fdb9`; two original uncorrected outputs showed 2/4096 f64 bits equal, max abs 0.0009765625003673919 / 0.00097656250081434, RMS 0.0004832420947045149 / 0.00046318433256012354. User proved private archive issue resolved. **DO NOT attribute these huge mismatches to differing r8brain versions.**
- **ROOT CAUSE OF THE STAGE40 COMPARISON ERROR (not native vendor mismatch):** original `coeff_post_fft` array was intercepted immediately after Ooura forward FFT (unmodified packed real/imag bins); public `CDSPFIRFilter::getKernelBlock()` returns FFT `after CDSPRealFFT::convertToZP()`. For !PFFFT implementation `convertToZP()` sets `p[i+1]=p[i]` at i=2,4,...,Len-2, deliberately duplicating the real spectrum into both slots for SIMD zero-phase multiplication. Stage40's previous direct `compare(public, ref)` compared unconverted **imaginary** values against duplicated **real** values for odd entries. This alone accounts for approximately 0.0009765625 peak error and near all missed f64 bits. Original result is **NOT a valid public-vs-vendor generated FIR spectrum comparison.** Stage39's 1972-f64 numerical convolution PASS unaffected.
- **INDEPENDENT REAL NATIVE VERIFICATION:** with the supplied 4-array private FIR ZIP, reconstruct the Ooura-packed raw forward FFT from `numpy.fft.rfft(coeff_pre_fft)` (negative NumPy imaginary sign). For both originals, max abs vs captured `coeff_post_fft` is **4.336808689942018e-19** (RMS 1.1994770827383831e-19 and 1.1796966723263094e-19). Applying `convertToZP()` to a copy of native raw spectrum yields each bin's duplicated real slot *bit-identical* across 2047 real/duplicate pairs. This confirms the format diagnosis without a new DLL run. Saved hashes/metrics in `research/htkpa/stages/stage40/evidence.json` (NO vendor buffers committed).
- **FIX COMMITTED:** `stages/stage40/audit.py` now checks native raw vs NumPy FFT, explicitly converts native to ZP `aligned_native[3::2]=aligned_native[2::2]`, and compares `public_vs_native_spectrum` only against aligned native. The old `INVALID_previous_public_vs_unconverted_native_spectrum` is retained solely as a diagnostic control; never use it for version inference. Stage40 README updated. **Corrected public-vs-native numerical parity PENDING USER WSL RERUN**; do not manufacture results.
- **NEXT:** user can rerun precisely the same Stage40 WSL command (with either known-good ZIP). Compare new `public_vs_native_spectrum` values at both filter cutoffs; *then* test pinned v6.5 / v7.1 / v7.2 under a format-aligned gate to distinguish historical source/compiler differences if warranted.

## 2026-10-14 — Stage46 static PE64 RTTI finds original `CDSPFracInterpolator::process` at 0x1800135C0 (NO FRIDA)

- **SOURCE IMAGE & SAFETY:** Used the user-supplied proprietary original native `5868USB.dll`, SHA256 `fa8b3613a75de69deb49a9e72dce453312e4814a5c99bb6ea2e8a9e80debd5fa`, in read-only offline disassembly and an MSVC PE64 RTTI/vftable scanner. Original vendor class `r8b::CDSPBlockConvolver` (`.?AVCDSPBlockConvolver@r8b@@`) has valid CompleteObjectLocator RVA `0x253f38` and vftable RVA `0x244ea0`; its virtual **slot 6 `process`** is `0x18000C220`, the previously captured Stage32/33/36 input entry. Original vendor class `r8b::CDSPFracInterpolator<...>` has MSVC RTTI `.?AV?$CDSPFracInterpolator@$0BI@$0CKB@@r8b@@`, COL RVA `0x253e68`, vftable RVA `0x245170`, virtual **slot 6 `process`** `0x1800135C0`. Vendor `CDSPResampler24` RTTI/vftable process slot6 `0x180011850`. All class vtable entries validated as pointers into executable PE `.text` region; pinned original SHA check. Upstream `CDSPProcessor.h` provides the 7 method order, control BlockConvolver site matches prior independent dynamic trace.
- **DISASSEMBLY CONVENTION:** original `0x1800135C0` starts `mov rax,rsp; push r12; push r13; sub rsp,38h; mov r12,[r9]; mov r13,r9; mov rsi,rdx; mov r15d,r8d` which agrees with MSVC x64 `process(this=RCX, input=RDX, input_count=R8D, output_double**=R9)` and return f64 count in `EAX`. `R9` is a **pointer to an output pointer** that can be replaced and is not directly an f64 array. Native RTTI is specialized templated `CDSPFracInterpolator<...>`, unlike public v6.5 non-template API; do not infer precise source revision from the numerical FIR match.

## 2026-10-14 — Stage47 STATIC: last-stage process return & pointer/capacity proven, still no vendor final waveform

- **TRUE OFFLINE ORIGINAL DLL STATIC STUDY:** analyzed exact original `5868USB.dll`, SHA256 `fa8b3613a75de69deb49a9e72dce453312e4814a5c99bb6ea2e8a9e80debd5fa`, with LLVM 17.0.0 `llvm-objdump`. Anchors independently checked by a local Python static verification: 4 wrapper, 4 last-stage buffer setup, 4 specialized fractional process and 1 clear/vftable anchors, all **PASS**, plus broader examination of wrapper 67 instructions / buffer setup 26 / fraction 347 / clear 11. No Frida, Manager, active native calls or proprietary bytes included in Git. Stage47 reproducibility script `stages/stage47/audit_static_return.py`, numerical/static findings `evidence.json`, detailed guardrails in README.
- **ORIGINAL `CDSPResampler24::process` wrapper VA `0x180011850`:** preserves caller `double*& op0` reference in `R15` at `0x18001185C`. Intermediate virtual `process` call at `0x1800118B1` uses local output-pointer variable `[RSP+0x40]`; its output count EAX and pointer are passed to subsequent processing. If `[RBX+0x50]` (last-stage instance) nonnull, wrapper selects final buffer `[RBX+0x70]` or falls back to original input pointer, saves it into caller's `*R15` at `0x1800118FA`, then calls final stage's vtable slot 6 `process` by `call *0x30(%rax)` **at `0x180011909`**. **`0x18001190C` is the first instruction after its return**, with output count in EAX and proposed actual output base in `*R15`. This is an **indirect LAST-stage return site**; static code alone does not establish it is reached by the particular 44.1→48 vendor run or that the dynamic receiver is the fractional class.
- **SPECIALIZED FRACTION PROCESS VA `0x1800135C0`:** entry reads `*R9` into R12 and saves R9 in R13. Writes each f64 via `movsd %xmm3,(%r12)` at `0x180013C38`, increments R12 by eight at `0x180013C3E`, computes `(R12-*R13)>>3` into return EAX at `0x180013D15–0x180013D1D`. Across 347 disassembled instructions **no CALL** and no direct write into saved `*R13` pointer-reference: only one dereference at `0x180013D15` reads it. This strongly favors the wrapper's before-call `*R15` output base remaining valid immediately after this specific final stage, while still requiring runtime lifetime and pointer guards.
- **DYNAMIC-TYPE STATIC CONTROL:** resampler `clear()` code at `0x180011A4F–0x180011A56` explicitly compares last-stage object vftable to original templated `CDSPFracInterpolator<...>` vftable `0x180245170` from Stage46. Thus the resampler knows this specialization, but this is not actual runtime dispatch proof.
- **OUTPUT CAPACITY SETUP STATICALLY VERIFIED:** initial construction at `0x18001222A` stores last processor instance in `[RBX+0x50]`; `0x180012237` calls its virtual slot 4 `getMaxOutLen`. Depending on available capacity, wrapper may reuse `[RBX+0x58]`, select zero `[RBX+0x70]` (later caller-input fallback), or allocate/reuse `[RBX+0x68]` then assign pointer at `[RBX+0x70]` via `0x180012263`. Therefore there is **NO FIXED native 48k output pointer**, and resulting memory may be overwritten on future process calls. Any read must occur immediately after that call, verify actual pointer, numeric length and dynamic last-stage type. No capacity verified at runtime yet.
- **STAGE47 result:** `STATIC_LAST_STAGE_RETURN_SITE_VERIFIED`. Candidate **`0x18001190C`** for FUTURE guarded *single metadata-only* callback, not a proven-safe hook. NEXT proper gate is module SHA + dynamic vftable `0x180245170` + bounded EAX + valid `*R15` and stable CLO completion. **No new vendor final 48k waveform captured, no Frida config executed or installed**. After a metadata-only successful run could consider existing Stage35-style one-hook `f64_binary` method at the same return site; NEVER revive known crash-prone `0x18000C220`+`0x18000C838` two-hook setups. Stage45 hybrid is PUBLIC fractional only, not vendor final parity. Production remains unchanged.

## 2026-10-14 — Frida workflow architectural correction: ONE universal /tools engine; stages hold config/evidence only

Individual stages should hold only the declarative parameters for that shared tool and source/evidence/output references. New future functionality should be added to `/tools` as a validated, opt-in feature; do not clone old Stage48 code for Stage49+.
- **IMPLEMENTED IN GITHUB:** `tools/frida_capture.py` remains the **only supported Frida CLI entrypoint** and retains its legacy scalar/f64 slice/block/binary capture modes. `tools/FRIDA_WSL.sh` is the **one canonical WSL entrypoint**, now accepts `--config research/htkpa/stages/stage48/frida.json` (or old `FRIDA_CONFIG`), colors the terminal, automatically reuses the existing Windows Frida Python, detects a single Sonicake process without hand-entered PID and locates private DLL. - **REPRO COMMANDS:** offline `python tools/frida_capture.py --self-test`, `python -m unittest discover -s tools -p 'test_frida_universal.py'`, `bash -n tools/FRIDA_WSL.sh`; then, only after offline checks pass and user opens Manager, `bash tools/FRIDA_WSL.sh --config research/htkpa/stages/stage48/frida.json`. ## 2026-10-14 — Universal Frida process lifecycle moves to stage JSON: app auto-start/owned close and exact CLO folder

- **USER REQUIREMENT:** Universal `/tools` Frida must itself open and close target application, with installed program EXE and CLO output folder specified **per stage in the JSON**, not by new bespoke Python/BAT scripts or hardcoded launcher arguments. Prior Stage48 expected manually opened Sonicake Manager only because the guarded observer originally implemented attach-only. The base universal legacy scalar runner already contained `--fresh` spawn logic, but guarded mode lacked lifecycle support and could not close its own GUI.
- **CANONICAL CODE UPDATED:** `tools/frida_guarded.py` now supports strictly validated optional `lifecycle` config with `executable`, `start=(fresh|if_missing|attach_only)`, `close=(if_launched|never)`, and bounded `close_timeout_seconds=1..30`. Config `completion` supports either user-specified absolute Windows `path` or prior `appdata_subdir` relative to Windows APPDATA. Path values reject traversal/unsafe components; no shell command execution or arbitrary JS accepted. Windows `--preflight` through generic `tools/frida_capture.py` checks the configured installed EXE and CLO directory along with the original DLL SHA **without Frida attach/spawn/hooks**. CLI `--config` remains the only stage selector. `tools/FRIDA_WSL.sh` is still the only WSL launcher, with colored startup/cleanup instructions.
- **GUARDED PROCESS START/STOP DESIGN:** `lifecycle.start=fresh` first REFUSES any matching preexisting process; no force-close or ownership confusion. It checks the configured EXE on disk and calls `frida.get_local_device().spawn([EXE])` to launch the manager suspended, attaches the one Frida module observer then resumes the newly spawned PID. The original loaded `5868USB.dll` backing-file SHA is still hashed BEFORE arming the single metadata site `RVA 0x1190C`. Cleanup is in a guarded finally: unload/detach the script and **send Windows WM_CLOSE only to top-level windows of the exact tool-spawned PID**, wait up to configured timeout, and preserve `cleanup_status` in `manifest.json`. It will NEVER force-kill any running/resumed GUI, or terminate a preexisting user instance. Only a tool-owned process that is still suspended because startup failed may be terminated. If graceful GUI close fails, report it explicitly (nonzero exit 3 for requested but unsuccessful managed closure) and leave app open. `if_missing` attaches to an existing Manager without closing it, or spawns/owns a new one; `attach_only` retains old behavior. This config mechanism is shared for future guarded stages and not restricted to a Stage48-specific runner.
- **STAGE48 JSON VALUES:** `research/htkpa/stages/stage48/frida.json` now contains `lifecycle.executable="C:/Program Files/Sonicake/Sonicake Manager/Sonicake Manager.exe"`, `start="fresh"`, `close="if_launched"`, `close_timeout_seconds=8`. **IMPORTANT**: the EXE path came from the universal runner's previous DEFAULT fallback; the user's *actual installed EXE path is NOT independently verified yet*. It must be changed in this JSON if Windows preflight fails. `completion.path="C:/Users/TheColdVoid/AppData/Roaming/Sonicake/Sonicake Manager"`, `recursive=true`, `stable_seconds=3`; this exact CLO folder was explicitly provided by the user and does not require guessing or APPDATA discovery. Stage README/doc updated.
- **NEW TESTS:** `tools/test_frida_universal.py` extended with explicit Stage48 paths, refusal of malformed lifecycle/config paths and unsafe process actions, guarded fresh launch refusing an existing user Manager, mocked Frida own-process spawn and cleanup ownership, graceful WM_CLOSE versus owned-suspended-only cancellation. Legacy unguarded f64 capture mode still uses its original semantics; no existing historic stage configs changed. Earlier 7/7 WSL tests confirmed the prior version, **new lifecycle changes are PREPARED AND HAVE NOT YET BEEN USER WSL/Windows EXECUTED**.
- **CANONICAL USER COMMANDS:** first `git pull --ff-only origin main`; `python tools/frida_capture.py --self-test`, `python -m unittest discover -s tools -p test_frida_universal.py -v`, `bash -n tools/FRIDA_WSL.sh`, then no-hook Windows path preflight `bash tools/FRIDA_WSL.sh --config research/htkpa/stages/stage48/frida.json --preflight`. After successful preflight with **Manager closed**, normal `bash tools/FRIDA_WSL.sh --config research/htkpa/stages/stage48/frida.json` should start its own Manager, print `[HOOK]` after DLL loaded/hashing/arming, permit one manually initiated NAM→CLO conversion, detect stable CLO, then gracefully request Manager close. Startup/shutdown automation DOES NOT itself click GUI or convert NAM; any missing EXE path must be reported instead of guessed/forced. - **Private primary artifact:** `frida_20261014_160153_049871_j0ax32i0.tar.gz` SHA256 `e23f13ba1aeaadb2a9889016f62945916e772279795b5ebf1f0112cb6b8c23ff`; actual independent `5868USB.dll` V1.5.2 SHA256 `fa8b3613a75de69deb49a9e72dce453312e4814a5c99bb6ea2e8a9e80debd5fa`. - **Corrected static interpretation (CRITICAL):** Previous claim that fractional `CDSPFracInterpolator::process` preserves `R8D` across the call is **FALSE**. SHA-matched DLL vtable `0x245170` dispatches process to RVA **`0x135C0`**. At `0x135DC` original `R8D` input count is copied into `R15D`; at `0x1366C` `R8` is overwritten as a temporary pointer; `0x13750` loads the internal ring index at `[R10+0x1028]` into `R8D`; `0x13C9E–0x13CA2` writes an updated low-byte index back to that field. Hence Stage51 `trace_return.input_f64` is a **mislabeled POSTCALL REGISTER VALUE, NOT INPUT COUNT**. Its numeric series includes `221,13,221,13,0,...,3,237,238,236,237`; the retained legacy JSON key is not a semantic guarantee. Live output pairs `(#18–21)=(114,118,349,1882)` remain valid independent `EAX` return counts.
- **Next gate:** Need actual fractional *entry* `R8D`, before callee clobbers it, together with paired *return* `EAX` from same invocation. Prepared strict single-hook no-audio Stage52 at RVA `0x135C0` (guarded vtable `0x245170`, 64 event maximum, SHA-gated). Do not infer absolute stream index or vendor/public numerical parity until the new inputs/outputs and waveform provenance align.

Relevant branch creates a **2:1 CDSPBlockConvolver** at RVA `0x11cfe`, stores at `resampler+0x8` with count 1 (`0x11d22/0x11d26`); conditional `2*src>dst` at `0x11d7c` proceeds via `0x11f1f` to a **final fractional interpolator** vftable RVA `0x245170` at `0x121ae` then `resampler+0x50` at `0x1222a`; runtime wrapper loops intermediate virtual `process` at `0x118b1`, then last stage at `0x11909`. **34/34 source opcode/operand anchors** independently checked against original binary using local LLVM Intel disassembler.
- **SOURCE CUTOFF:** For dst≤src the native FIR generator is passed `NormFreq=0.5*dst/src` (RVAs `0x11c90,0x11c94,0x11c99,0x11ce3`; native PE constant `0x24c880` = double 0.5). Frequency-domain performance and this specific live parent are not established by constructor source alone.
